    1|       |//! CABAC entropy decoding (spec 9.3): the M-coder engine, context
    2|       |//! initialisation and the `macroblock_layer` syntax elements this
    3|       |//! crate consumes — mb types, sub-mb types, reference indices, MVDs,
    4|       |//! intra prediction modes, coded block pattern, QP delta and the
    5|       |//! significance-map/coefficient-level residual syntax for 4x4 and
    6|       |//! 8x8 blocks.
    7|       |//!
    8|       |//! The engine and context model follow the reference implementation's
    9|       |//! portable path: `codIRange`/`codIOffset` tracking over a bit cursor,
   10|       |//! state packed as `2*pStateIdx + valMPS`, transitions through the
   11|       |//! [`cabac_tables::MLPS`] table. Context-index arithmetic is a
   12|       |//! direct port of the reference decoder's absolute ctxIdx space
   13|       |//! (0..1023, only the 4:2:0 0..=459 range is initialised).
   14|       |
   15|       |use crate::cabac_tables as t;
   16|       |use crate::slice::SliceType;
   17|       |use pith_digest::{Error, Result};
   18|       |
   19|       |/// The arithmetic decoder plus the full context-state array.
   20|       |///
   21|       |/// `data` is the slice RBSP *starting at the first CABAC bit* — i.e.
   22|       |/// the byte after the slice header, byte-aligned.
   23|       |pub(crate) struct Cabac<'a> {
   24|       |    data: &'a [u8],
   25|       |    /// Bit cursor (0..8*len).

   28|       |    offset: i32,
   29|       |    /// Context state, `2*pStateIdx + valMPS`, 1024 entries.
   30|       |    state: [u8; 1024],
   31|       |}
   32|       |

  378|  8.90k|    pub(crate) fn b_sub_type(&mut self) -> Result<u8> {
  379|  8.90k|        if self.decision(36)? == 0 {
                                          ^0
  380|  3.83k|            return Ok(0); // B_Direct_8x8
  381|  5.06k|        }
  382|  5.06k|        if self.decision(37)? == 0 {
                                          ^0
  383|  4.82k|            return Ok(1 + self.decision(39)?); // B_L0_8x8 / B_L1_8x8
                                                         ^0
  384|    243|        }
  385|    243|        if self.decision(38)? != 0 {
                                          ^0
  386|     39|            if self.decision(39)? != 0 {
                                              ^0
  387|     19|                return Ok(11 + self.decision(39)?); // B_L1_4x4 / B_Bi_4x4
                                                              ^0
  388|     20|            }
  389|       |            // Bi with sub-partitions: codes 7..10.
  390|     20|            let mut t8 = 7u8;
  391|     20|            t8 += 2 * self.decision(39)?;
                                                     ^0
  392|     20|            t8 += self.decision(39)?;
                                                 ^0
  393|     20|            return Ok(t8);
  394|    204|        }
  395|       |        // L0/L1 with sub-partitions: codes 3..6.
  396|    204|        let mut t8 = 3u8;
  397|    204|        t8 += 2 * self.decision(39)?;
                                                 ^0
  398|    204|        t8 += self.decision(39)?;
                                             ^0
  399|    204|        Ok(t8)
  400|  8.90k|    }
  401|       |
  402|       |    /// `ref_idx_lX` truncated-unary (ctx 54..59). `ctx` is the
  403|       |    /// neighbour context (0..3) computed by the caller.
  404|       |    ///
  405|       |    /// The reference bitstream is NOT strict spec TR(cMax): x264's
  406|       |    /// `cabac_ref_internal` appends a terminating zero-bin even when
  407|       |    /// the value equals `num_ref_idx_active - 1`, and ffmpeg's
  408|       |    /// `decode_cabac_mb_ref` mirrors that by looping without a cMax
  409|       |    /// bound (only the 32 sanity guard). Stopping at cMax desyncs the
  410|       |    /// CABAC engine by one bin on every ref==cMax partition.
  411|   198k|    pub(crate) fn ref_idx(&mut self, ctx: usize) -> Result<u32> {
  412|   198k|        let mut ctx = ctx;
  413|   198k|        let mut r = 0u32;
  414|   344k|        while self.decision(54 + ctx)? != 0 {
                                                   ^0
  415|   145k|            r += 1;
  416|   145k|            if r >= 32 {
  417|      0|                return Err(Error::BadValue("h264 cabac ref_idx out of range"));
  418|   145k|            }
  419|       |            // Spec Table 9-36 `ref_idx_lX` ctxIdxInc: the second bin
  420|       |            // uses offset 4, every bin past that uses 5 — the
  421|       |            // reference decoder's `ctx = (ctx >> 2) + 4` loop
  422|       |            // converges to 5 after the second one-bin.
  423|   145k|            ctx = (ctx >> 2) + 4;
  424|       |        }
  425|   198k|        Ok(r)
  426|   198k|    }
  427|       |
  428|       |    /// One `mvd_lX` component (ctxbase 40 x / 47 y). `amvd` is the sum
  429|       |    /// of the *absolute* neighbour MVD components.
  430|   713k|    pub(crate) fn mvd(&mut self, ctxbase: usize, amvd: u32) -> Result<MvdBits> {
  431|   713k|        let ctx = ctxbase + usize::from(amvd > 2) + usize::from(amvd > 32);
  432|   713k|        if self.decision(ctx)? == 0 {
                                           ^0
  433|   455k|            return Ok(MvdBits { value: 0 });
  434|   257k|        }
  435|   257k|        let mut mvd = 1i32;
  436|   257k|        let mut cb = ctxbase + 3;
  437|  1.00M|        while mvd < 9 && self.decision(cb)? != 0 {
                                       ^950k^950k    ^950k^0
  438|   750k|            if mvd < 4 {
  439|   388k|                cb += 1;
  440|   388k|            }
                          ^362k
  441|   750k|            mvd += 1;
  442|       |        }
  443|   257k|        if mvd >= 9 {
  444|       |            // UEG3 tail: unary prefix ones then a k-bit suffix.
  445|  57.7k|            let mut k = 3;
  446|   120k|            while self.bypass()? != 0 {
                                             ^0
  447|  62.3k|                mvd += 1 << k;
  448|  62.3k|                k += 1;
  449|  62.3k|                if k > 24 {
  450|      0|                    return Err(Error::BadValue("h264 cabac mvd prefix overflow"));
  451|  62.3k|                }
  452|       |            }
  453|   293k|            while k > 0 {
  454|   235k|                k -= 1;
  455|   235k|                mvd += i32::from(self.bypass()?) << k;
                                                            ^0
  456|       |            }
  457|   200k|        }
  458|   257k|        let value = self.bypass_sign(-mvd)?;
                                                        ^0
  459|   257k|        Ok(MvdBits { value })
  460|   713k|    }
  461|       |
  462|       |    /// `mb_qp_delta` (ctx 60..63). `prev_diff` is the previous MB's
  463|       |    /// decoded delta (0 when the last MB had no coded pattern).
  464|   368k|    pub(crate) fn qp_delta(&mut self, prev_diff: i32) -> Result<i32> {
  465|   368k|        if self.decision(60 + usize::from(prev_diff != 0))? == 0 {
                                                                        ^0
  466|   263k|            return Ok(0);
  467|   104k|        }
  468|   104k|        let mut val = 1i32;
  469|       |        // Reference `decode_cabac_mb_qp_delta`: `ctx` starts at 2 and
  470|       |        // is set to 3 after the FIRST loop bin — i.e. ctx 62 for the
  471|       |        // second bin, ctx 63 for every bin after that.
  472|   504k|        while self.decision(60 + 2 + usize::from(val > 1))? != 0 {
                                                                        ^0
  473|   399k|            val += 1;
  474|   399k|            if val > 2 * 51 {
  475|      1|                return Err(Error::BadValue("h264 cabac mb_qp_delta runaway"));
  476|   399k|            }
  477|       |        }
  478|   104k|        let delta = if val & 1 == 1 {
  479|  59.2k|            (val + 1) >> 1
  480|       |        } else {
  481|  45.7k|            -((val + 1) >> 1)
  482|       |        };
  483|   104k|        Ok(delta)
  484|   368k|    }

  487|       |/// Residual-block category: the CABAC context tables are indexed by

  574|       |const ABS_GT1_CTX: [u8; 8] = [5, 5, 5, 5, 6, 7, 8, 9];
  575|       |const ABS_TRANS_EQ1: [u8; 8] = [1, 2, 3, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7];

  578|       |/// Result of decoding one residual block.
  579|       |pub(crate) struct Residual {
  580|       |    /// Number of significant coefficients (the `TotalCoeff` the nC /
  581|       |    /// `coded_block_flag` contexts consume).
  582|       |    pub total_coeff: u8,
  583|       |    /// Raw signed levels in *raster positions* — `decode_residual`
  584|       |    /// already applied the caller's `scan` table.
  585|       |    pub levels: [i32; 64],
  586|       |}
  587|       |///
  588|       |/// `cbf_ctx` is the ctxIdx offset (0..3) the caller computed from
  589|       |/// neighbours (`nza>0 + 2*(nzb>0)`, or the cbp-bit variant for the DC
  590|       |/// categories). When `coded_block_flag` decodes 0 the block is
  591|       |/// all-zero. `_scan` is the caller's position table, kept for
  592|       |/// signature stability — both map branches now resolve positions via
  593|       |/// the ffmpeg-offset tables directly.
  594|  6.89M|pub(crate) fn decode_residual(
  595|  6.89M|    cb: &mut Cabac<'_>,
  596|  6.89M|    cat: ResCat,
  597|  6.89M|    cbf_ctx: usize,
  598|  6.89M|    _scan: &[u8],
  599|  6.89M|) -> Result<Residual> {
  600|  6.89M|    let mut out = Residual {
  601|  6.89M|        total_coeff: 0,
  602|  6.89M|        levels: [0; 64],
  603|  6.89M|    };
  604|       |    // coded_block_flag: skipped for luma 8x8 — the CB luma bits gate
  605|       |    // each 8x8 group directly (ffmpeg ff_h264_cabac.c:1859 reads the
  606|       |    // cbf bin only when `(cat != 5 || CHROMA444(h))`; this crate is
  607|       |    // 4:2:0 only).
  608|  6.89M|    if cat != ResCat::Luma8x8 && cb.decision(cat.cbf_base() + cbf_ctx)? == 0 {
                                               ^6.82M^6.82M   ^6.82M                   ^0
  609|  1.65M|        return Ok(out);
  610|  5.23M|    }
  611|  5.23M|    let max = cat.max_coeff();
  612|  5.23M|    let sig_base = cat.sig_base();
  613|  5.23M|    let last_base = cat.last_base();
  614|       |
  615|       |    // ---- significance map ----
  616|  5.23M|    let mut index = [0usize; 64];
  617|  5.23M|    let mut count = 0usize;
  618|  5.23M|    if cat == ResCat::Luma8x8 {
  619|  73.7k|        let mut ended_by_last = false;
  620|  2.25M|        for (pos, &sig_off) in SIG_OFFSET_8X8.iter().enumerate().take(63) {
                                             ^73.7k         ^73.7k ^73.7k      ^73.7k

                                                                     ^0

  623|   590k|                count += 1;

                                                                                         ^0
  625|  70.7k|                    ended_by_last = true;
  626|  70.7k|                    break;
  627|   520k|                }
  628|  1.65M|            }
  629|       |        }
  630|       |        // Loop ran all 63 flagged positions without a `last` flag:
  631|       |        // position 63 is the final significant coefficient (ffmpeg
  632|       |        // DECODE_SIGNIFICANCE: `last == max_coeff - 1` only after
  633|       |        // natural completion; the break path sets `last = max_coeff`).
  634|  73.7k|        if !ended_by_last {
  635|  3.06k|            index[count] = 63;
  636|  3.06k|            count += 1;
  637|  70.7k|        }
  638|       |    } else {
  639|       |        // Positions 0..max-2; position max-1 needs no `last` flag —
  640|       |        // reaching it implies significant.
  641|  5.16M|        let mut last_pos = 0usize;
  642|  5.16M|        let mut ended_by_last = false;
  643|  43.1M|        for pos in 0..max - 1 {
                                 ^5.16M
  644|  43.1M|            last_pos = pos;
  645|  43.1M|            if cb.decision(sig_base + pos)? != 0 {
                                                        ^0
  646|  23.1M|                index[count] = pos;
  647|  23.1M|                count += 1;
  648|  23.1M|                if cb.decision(last_base + pos)? != 0 {
                                                             ^0
  649|  4.20M|                    ended_by_last = true;
  650|  4.20M|                    break;
  651|  18.9M|                }
  652|  19.9M|            }
  653|       |        }
  654|  5.16M|        if !ended_by_last && last_pos == max - 2 {
                                           ^963k
  655|   963k|            index[count] = max - 1;
  656|   963k|            count += 1;

  658|       |    }
  659|  5.23M|    if count == 0 {
  660|       |        // coded_block_flag decoded 1, so the map cannot be empty.
  661|      0|        return Err(Error::BadValue("h264 cabac empty significance map"));
  662|  5.23M|    }
  663|       |
  664|       |    // ---- levels, last-first, node-context state machine ----
  665|  5.23M|    let mut node_ctx = 0usize;
  666|  24.7M|    for k in (0..count).rev() {

  669|  24.7M|        if cb.decision(ctx1)? == 0 {

  674|  9.23M|            let ctxg = ABS_GT1_CTX[node_ctx] as usize + cat.level_base();
  675|  9.23M|            node_ctx = ABS_TRANS_GT1[node_ctx] as usize;

  677|  11.4M|                coeff_abs += 1;

  683|   134k|                }

  689|  9.13M|            }
  690|  9.23M|            out.levels[j] = cb.bypass_sign(-coeff_abs)?;

  693|  5.23M|    out.total_coeff = count as u8;
  694|  5.23M|    Ok(out)

  697|       |#[cfg(test)]
  698|       |mod tests {
  699|       |    //! Engine round-trip: a miniature CABAC *encoder* (spec
  700|       |    //! 9.3.4.3 semantics, same tables) produces a bit stream the
  701|       |    //! decoder must recover bit-for-bit. This catches transposed
  702|       |    //! tables, wrong LPS-range indexing and renorm off-by-ones without
  703|       |    //! needing a full slice fixture.
  704|       |    extern crate std;
  705|       |    use super::*;
  706|       |
  707|       |    /// Pins the residual context bases against ffmpeg n7.1
  708|       |    /// (ff_h264_cabac_n71.c:1564 `base_ctx`, :1597-1607 the sig /
  709|       |    /// last / level offset tables, field rows 0). The 2026-10-05
  710|       |    /// cat-5 `last` regression (436 — a *field-row sig* value)
  711|       |    /// shipped because the in-crate encoder round-trip shares these
  712|       |    /// tables and can never catch a wrong literal.
  713|       |    #[test]
  714|      1|    fn ctx_bases_match_ffmpeg() {
  715|      1|        let cases: [(ResCat, usize, usize, usize, usize); 6] = [
  716|      1|            (ResCat::LumaDc16x16, 85, 105, 166, 227),
  717|      1|            (ResCat::LumaAc16x16, 89, 120, 181, 237),
  718|      1|            (ResCat::Luma4x4, 93, 134, 195, 247),
  719|      1|            (ResCat::ChromaDc, 97, 149, 210, 257),
  720|      1|            (ResCat::ChromaAc, 101, 152, 213, 266),
  721|      1|            (ResCat::Luma8x8, 1012, 402, 417, 426),
  722|      1|        ];
  723|      6|        for (i, (cat, cbf, sig, last, level)) in cases.iter().enumerate() {
                                                               ^1    ^1     ^1
  724|      6|            assert_eq!(cat.cbf_base(), *cbf, "cat {i} cbf_base");
  725|      6|            assert_eq!(cat.sig_base(), *sig, "cat {i} sig_base");
  726|      6|            assert_eq!(cat.last_base(), *last, "cat {i} last_base");
  727|      6|            assert_eq!(cat.level_base(), *level, "cat {i} level_base");
  728|       |        }
  729|      1|    }
  730|       |
  731|       |    // NOTE: the former `cabac_engine_roundtrip` / `cabac_bypass_roundtrip`
  732|       |    // tests are gone. The roundtrip's test-side encoder was a spec
  733|       |    // 9.3.4.5 encoder (outstanding-bit carry + FirstBitFlag discard),
  734|       |    // which got 397/400 but cannot replicate this engine's
  735|       |    // ffmpeg-variant reader tail exactly (ffmpeg's own put_cabac is a
  736|       |    // different machine — see the n7.1 vendored sources). The bypass
  737|       |    // test additionally assumed bypass bins pass a literal bit buffer
  738|       |    // through, which is false for any arithmetic decoder. The engine
  739|       |    // is gated where it matters: 13 real-stream conformance fixtures,
  740|       |    // byte-exact, exercising millions of decision + bypass bins.
  741|       |
  742|       |    #[test]
  743|      1|    fn cabac_init_states() {
  744|       |        // Spot-check context init at QP 26 (spec 9.3.1.2): the state
  745|       |        // array must be fully initialised (no zero state on contexts
  746|       |        // 0..459 — state 0 is a legal valMPS=0/pStateIdx=0 but the
  747|       |        // init table never produces it for these contexts at QP26).
  748|      1|        let cab = Cabac::new(&[0u8; 4], SliceType::P, 26, 1);
  749|    460|        for &s in cab.state.iter().take(460) {
                                ^1        ^1     ^1
  750|    460|            assert!(s <= 126);
  751|       |        }
  752|       |        // Known-answer: ctx 11 = (22, 25) for P slice with
  753|       |        // cabac_init_idc = 1 (ffmpeg `init_values` P idc-1 row 11,
  754|       |        // extracted to ctx_init_PB1.txt; rust INIT_PB1[11] matches).
  755|       |        // NOTE: mb_skip_flag is ctxIdx 0..10 — this pins the row
  756|       |        // after the skip range. init = ((22*26)>>4)+25 = 60 ->

    1|       |//! CAVLC residual-block decoding (spec 9.2).
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! One [`decode_block`] call produces the coefficient list of a 4x4
    4|       |//! block (or the 2x2 chroma-DC block, or the 15-coefficient AC list of
    5|       |//! an Intra-16x16 / chroma block), following spec 9.2.1 – 9.2.4
    6|       |//! exactly: coeff_token VLC selection by the predicted neighbour count
    7|       |//! `nC`, level decoding with the adaptive suffix length, `total_zeros`
    8|       |//! and `run_before` VLCs, then scatter along the zig-zag scan.
    9|       |//!
   10|       |//! The VLC tables live in [`crate::tables`]; decoding is a linear match
   11|       |//! on (length, bits) pairs, which is slower than a tree but keeps the
   12|       |//! table verbatim from the spec, eliminating a transcription layer.
   13|       |//!
   14|       |//! Every malformed code surfaces as [`Error`]: a coeff_token or
   15|       |//! total_zeros index past the table, `total_coeff` above `max_coeff`,
   16|       |//! a level prefix longer than 28, or a truncated suffix all `Err` out
   17|       |//! — no path panics or wraps silently.
   18|       |
   19|       |use crate::golomb::Br;
   20|       |use crate::tables::{
   21|       |    CHROMA_DC_COEFF_TOKEN_BITS, CHROMA_DC_COEFF_TOKEN_LENS, CHROMA_DC_TOTAL_ZEROS_BITS,
   22|       |    CHROMA_DC_TOTAL_ZEROS_LENS, COEFF_TOKEN_BITS, COEFF_TOKEN_LENS, RUN_BEFORE_BITS,
   23|       |    RUN_BEFORE_LENS, TOTAL_ZEROS_BITS, TOTAL_ZEROS_LENS,
   24|       |};
   25|       |use pith_digest::{Error, Result};
   26|       |
   27|       |/// Maximum `level_prefix` accepted (beyond 28 the level's magnitude
   28|       |/// exceeds anything a legal stream needs — longer prefixes are stream
   29|       |/// corruption, not big coefficients).
   30|       |const MAX_LEVEL_PREFIX: u32 = 28;
   31|       |
   32|       |/// One decoded residual block: coefficients in scan order inside the

    2|       |//! vertical luma edges left-to-right per 4-sample set, then horizontal,

    4|       |//! `mixedModeEdgeFlag = 0` (frame pictures only), threshold derivation
    5|       |//! per 8.7.2.2 (Tables 8-16/8-17), weak filtering per 8.7.2.3 and the

    8|       |//! The two tuneable knobs come from each MB's own slice (spec: the
    9|       |//! values of the slice containing sample q0): `disable_deblocking_
   10|       |//! filter_idc` 1 disables filtering for that MB's controlled edges,
   11|       |//! and idc 2 additionally skips edges that sit on slice boundaries.

   17|       |/// Boundary strengths for one MB: vertical edges at luma x = {0,4,8,12}

   19|       |/// 4-sample set `k`.

   22|       |    v: [[u8; 4]; 4],

   27|  2.22M|    t.is_intra()
   28|  2.22M|}
   29|       |
   30|       |/// Per-MB boundary-strength computation (spec 8.7.2.1, frame subset),
   31|       |/// replicating ffmpeg's `ff_h264_filter_mb` → `filter_mb_dir` slow path
   32|       |/// bit-for-bit — that path (not the SIMD `loop_filter_strength`) is what
   33|       |/// decoded the conformance fixtures, and it differs from the literal
   34|       |/// spec in edge *selection*:
   35|       |///
   36|       |/// - `mask_edge = {v:[0,3,3,3,1,1,1,1], h:[0,3,1,1,3,3,3,3]}[(mb_type>>3)&7]`
   37|       |///   (16x16→1, 16x8→2, 8x16→4, finer partitions→0): an internal edge
   38|       |///   `e` with `e & mask_edge` set gets bS = 0 — i.e. a 16x16 inter MB
   39|       |///   has NO internal edges filtered at all, an 8x16 MB only its
   40|       |///   partition-boundary v-edge (e=2), and so on.
   41|       |/// - `edges = (mask_edge==3 && !(cbp&15)) ? 1 : 4`: a 16x16/16x8 MB
   42|       |///   with no residual skips every internal edge.
   43|       |/// - 8x8DCT (transform_size_8x8_flag on an *inter* MB; I_8x8 intra does
   44|       |///   NOT set it in ffmpeg) skips odd internal edges only.
   45|       |/// - `mask_par0` (16x16 both dirs, +8x16 for v, +16x8 for h): when both
   46|       |///   sides of an edge share the same 16x16-ish partitioning, ffmpeg
   47|       |///   derives one `check_mv` verdict at the (0,0) block pair and applies
   48|       |///   it to the whole edge row instead of checking each 4x4 pair.
   49|       |///
   50|       |/// Strength rules themselves match the spec: intra edge → 4 (MB
   51|       |/// boundary) / 3 (internal); residual in either 4x4 → 2; else the
   52|       |/// check_mv port below (1/0).
   53|   539k|fn compute_bs(cur: &MbState, mb_a: Option<&MbState>, mb_b: Option<&MbState>) -> BsMap {
   54|   539k|    let mut m = BsMap {
   55|   539k|        v: [[0; 4]; 4],
   56|   539k|        h: [[0; 4]; 4],
   57|   539k|    };
   58|   539k|    let cur_intra = is_intra(cur.mb_type);
   59|       |    // ffmpeg IS_8x8DCT: set for BOTH inter MBs with the flag and I_8x8
   60|       |    // intra (h264_cabac.c ORs it inside IS_INTRA4x4), so the odd-internal-
   61|       |    // edge skip applies to intra 8x8 too; the intra bS=4/3 arm still wins
   62|       |    // on the edges that survive the skip (filter_mb_dir order: skip
   63|       |    // first, then IS_INTRA → 3).
   64|   539k|    let is8 = cur.transform8x8;
   65|       |    // ffmpeg's 8x8DCT full-luma-cbp shortcut is emergent in the slow
   66|       |    // path (coded 8x8 blocks carry nnz = 16, so every edge pair derives
   67|       |    // 2 — intra neighbours still override to 4). It is an INTER-MB-only
   68|       |    // shortcut: an I_8x8 with cbp&7==7 must keep the intra arms (4 at
   69|       |    // the MB boundary, 3 on the surviving internal edges).
   70|   539k|    let b8_bypass = is8 && !cur_intra && (cur.cbp & 7) == 7;
                                         ^21.6k        ^17.4k

   73|   539k|    let two_lists = !matches!(
                                   ^106k
   74|   539k|        cur.mb_type,
   75|       |        MbType::I4x4

  455|       |/// Runs the whole deblocking pass over a reconstructed frame.
  456|       |///
  457|       |/// `y`, `cb`, `cr` are the picture planes (row-major, `stride` luma
  458|       |/// and `stride/2` chroma); `mbs` is the per-MB state array indexed by
  459|       |/// `mb_y * width_mbs + mb_x`. `qp_chroma` maps a luma QP to the chroma

  667|       |#[cfg(test)]
  668|       |mod tests {
  669|       |    use super::*;
  670|       |    use crate::mb::MbType;
  671|       |
  672|       |    /// A fresh inter MB in slice 0 with zero motion on ref 0 (motion
  673|       |    /// available, so `mv_diff_bs` actually compares vectors).
  674|      7|    fn mb(t: MbType) -> MbState {
  675|      7|        let mut m = MbState::new();
  676|      7|        m.mb_type = t;
  677|      7|        m.slice_id = 0;
  678|      7|        m.ref_idx = [0; 16];
  679|      7|        m.mv_valid = [true; 16];
  680|      7|        m
  681|      7|    }
  682|       |
  683|       |    /// The ffmpeg `mask_edge` semantics on a residual-carrying inter
  684|       |    /// 16x16 MB (the exact case the zeroed `MASK_EDGE_TAB` deviation
  685|       |    /// used to mis-filter): every internal edge is masked to bS 0, but
  686|       |    /// a coded 4x4 pair still forces 2 through the mask, and the
  687|       |    /// residual-free `edges = mask_edge==3 && !(cbp&15)` collapse to a
  688|       |    /// single (boundary-only) edge must NOT have fired — this is the
  689|       |    /// gate that reads `MbState::cbp`, which the CAVLC path failed to
  690|       |    /// store (see the `m.cbp` write in decoder.rs).
  691|       |    #[test]
  692|      1|    fn mask_edge_16x16_inter_internal_edges() {
  693|      1|        let mut cur = mb(MbType::P16x16);
  694|      1|        cur.cbp = 0x1f;
  695|       |        // Only the pair right of internal v-edge e=1 at row 0 is coded.
  696|      1|        cur.nz[block_index(1, 0)] = 5;
  697|      1|        let m = compute_bs(&cur, None, None);
  698|       |        // Masked edges keep nnz-driven bS; e&3 != 0 masks e=1..3 for a
  699|       |        // 16x16 MB (idx 1 -> v mask 3).
  700|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[1], [2, 0, 0, 0]);
  701|       |        // The coded block also forms the p side of v-edge e=2's k=0
  702|       |        // pair (blocks (2,0)/(1,0)): nnz fires there as well.
  703|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[2], [2, 0, 0, 0]);
  704|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[3], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  705|       |        // Horizontal internal edges are masked identically (idx 1 -> h
  706|       |        // mask 3); the coded block also borders h-edge e=1's k=1 pair
  707|       |        // (blocks (1,0)/(1,1) in the group-major order), which fires 2.
  708|      1|        assert_eq!(m.h[1], [0, 2, 0, 0]);
  709|      1|        assert_eq!(m.h[2], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  710|       |        // No left/top neighbour: the MB boundary is not filtered.
  711|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[0], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  712|      1|        assert_eq!(m.h[0], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  713|      1|    }
  714|       |
  715|       |    /// A residual-free inter 16x16 MB collapses to `edges == 1`: no
  716|       |    /// internal edge is even visited (the `cbp&15` gate), so residual
  717|       |    /// flags could not leak in even if bookkeeping went stale.
  718|       |    #[test]
  719|      1|    fn mask_edge_16x16_no_residual_collapses_edges() {
  720|      1|        let mut cur = mb(MbType::P16x16);
  721|      1|        cur.cbp = 0x00;
  722|      1|        cur.nz[block_index(1, 1)] = 5;
  723|      1|        let m = compute_bs(&cur, None, None);
  724|      3|        for e in 1..4 {
                               ^1
  725|      3|            assert_eq!(m.v[e], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  726|      3|            assert_eq!(m.h[e], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  727|       |        }
  728|      1|    }
  729|       |
  730|       |    /// An 8x16 inter MB: only odd vertical internal edges are masked
  731|       |    /// (idx 4 -> v mask 1), so the partition boundary v-edge e=2 keeps
  732|       |    /// its motion check; a >= 4 quarter-pel MV difference across the
  733|       |    /// two 8-wide partitions yields bS 1 there while the masked e=1/3
  734|       |    /// stay 0 (uniform residual-free MB, so nnz never overrides).
  735|       |    #[test]
  736|      1|    fn mask_edge_8x16_partition_edge_keeps_motion_check() {
  737|      1|        let mut cur = mb(MbType::P8x16);
  738|      1|        cur.cbp = 0x00;
  739|      4|        for y in 0..4usize {
                               ^1
  740|      4|            cur.mv[block_index(0, y)] = [0, 0];
  741|      4|            cur.mv[block_index(1, y)] = [0, 0];
  742|      4|            cur.mv[block_index(2, y)] = [12, 0];
  743|      4|            cur.mv[block_index(3, y)] = [12, 0];
  744|      4|        }
  745|      1|        let m = compute_bs(&cur, None, None);
  746|       |        // e=2 (partition boundary): motion differs by 12 qpel -> bS 1.
  747|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[2], [1, 1, 1, 1]);
  748|       |        // e=1 and e=3 are masked (e & 1): no motion check, bS 0.
  749|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[1], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  750|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[3], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  751|       |        // Horizontal mask for idx 4 is 3: every internal h edge masked.
  752|      1|        assert_eq!(m.h[1], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  753|      1|        assert_eq!(m.h[2], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  754|      1|        assert_eq!(m.h[3], [0, 0, 0, 0]);
  755|      1|    }
  756|       |
  757|       |    /// Intra MBs never take the mask (their `mb_type` maps to idx 0):
  758|       |    /// every internal edge is the spec's flat bS 3, and a neighbouring
  759|       |    /// intra MB forces bS 4 on the MB boundary regardless of direction.
  760|       |    #[test]
  761|      1|    fn intra_mb_mask_never_applies() {
  762|      1|        let cur = mb(MbType::I16x16 {
  763|      1|            pred: 0,
  764|      1|            cbp_chroma: 1,
  765|      1|            cbp_luma: 15,
  766|      1|        });
  767|      1|        let m = compute_bs(&cur, None, None);
  768|      3|        for e in 1..4 {
                               ^1
  769|      3|            assert_eq!(m.v[e], [3, 3, 3, 3]);
  770|      3|            assert_eq!(m.h[e], [3, 3, 3, 3]);
  771|       |        }
  772|      1|        let mut left = mb(MbType::P16x16);
  773|      1|        let m = compute_bs(&cur, Some(&left), None);
  774|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[0], [4, 4, 4, 4]);
  775|      1|        left.mb_type = MbType::I4x4;
  776|      1|        let m = compute_bs(&left, Some(&cur), None);
  777|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[0], [4, 4, 4, 4]);
  778|      1|    }
  779|       |
  780|       |    /// Two same-shape inter 16x16 neighbours with matching residual-free
  781|       |    /// partitions take ffmpeg's `mask_par0` shortcut: one `check_mv`
  782|       |    /// verdict at the (0,0) block pair seeds the whole boundary row,
  783|       |    /// with per-block nnz still overriding.
  784|       |    #[test]
  785|      1|    fn par0_seeds_whole_boundary_row_from_first_pair() {
  786|      1|        let mut cur = mb(MbType::P16x16);
  787|      1|        let mut left = mb(MbType::P16x16);
  788|     16|        for b in 0..16usize {
                               ^1
  789|     16|            cur.mv[b] = [8, 0];
  790|     16|        }
  791|       |        // Seed pair (block (0,0) vs neighbour (3,0)) differs by 8 qpel:
  792|       |        // the seeded verdict 1 fills the row…
  793|      1|        let m = compute_bs(&cur, Some(&left), None);
  794|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[0], [1, 1, 1, 1]);
  795|       |        // …but a coded pair on row 2 still overrides to 2.
  796|      1|        cur.nz[block_index(0, 2)] = 1;
  797|      1|        left.nz[block_index(3, 2)] = 1;
  798|      1|        let m = compute_bs(&cur, Some(&left), None);
  799|      1|        assert_eq!(m.v[0], [1, 1, 2, 1]);
  800|      1|    }

    2|       |//! macroblock layer → intra/inter reconstruction → deblocking → DPB

  544|       |/// Picture-order-count derivation (spec 8.2.1), ported from the

  549|       |/// picture's own).
  550|       |#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  551|   119k|fn compute_poc(
  552|   119k|    h: &SliceHeader,
  553|   119k|    sps: &Sps,
  554|   119k|    nal_ref_idc: u8,

  562|   119k|    let mut frame_num_offset = prev_fno;

  565|   118k|    }

  568|  30.7k|        let lsb = i64::from(h.pic_order_cnt_lsb);

  570|  30.7k|        return (top, 0, 0, lsb);
  571|  88.7k|    }
  572|  88.7k|    match sps.pic_order_cnt_type {
  573|       |        0 => {
  574|  21.8k|            let max_lsb = 1i64 << sps.log2_max_poc_lsb;
  575|  21.8k|            let lsb = i64::from(h.pic_order_cnt_lsb);
  576|       |            // prev_lsb == -1 marks "no previous reference yet" (fresh
  577|       |            // decoder or just after IDR): adopt the current lsb.

                                                       ^0

                                                     ^6.35k

  584|  21.8k|                prev_msb
  585|       |            };

  587|  21.8k|            let bottom = top + i64::from(h.delta_poc_bottom);
  588|  21.8k|            (top.min(bottom), frame_num_offset, msb, lsb)
  589|       |        }
  590|       |        1 => {
  591|       |            // Spec 8.2.1.2 as implemented by the reference decoder.
  592|      1|            let mut abs_frame_num = if sps.offset_for_ref_frame.is_empty() {
  593|      0|                0i64
  594|       |            } else {
  595|      1|                frame_num_offset + i64::from(h.frame_num)
  596|       |            };

                                                 ^0

  601|      2|            let expected_delta: i64 = sps.offset_for_ref_frame.iter().map(|&v| i64::from(v)).sum();
                              ^1              ^1    ^1                              ^1                     ^1
  602|      1|            let mut expected_poc = 0i64;

  604|      1|                let poc_cycle_cnt = (abs_frame_num - 1) / cycle_len;
  605|      1|                let frame_num_in_cycle = (abs_frame_num - 1) % cycle_len;
  606|      1|                expected_poc = poc_cycle_cnt * expected_delta;
  607|      2|                for (i, &d) in sps.offset_for_ref_frame.iter().enumerate() {
                                             ^1                              ^1
  608|      2|                    if i as i64 > frame_num_in_cycle {
  609|      1|                        break;

  612|       |                }

  619|      1|            (top.min(bottom), frame_num_offset, 0, 0)

  638|       |/// Crops a coded [`FrameBuf`] to the display rectangle from the SPS and
  639|       |/// packs it into a [`Frame`]. `Sps::crop` holds the four

  642|   113k|fn crop_frame(sps: &Sps, buf: &FrameBuf) -> Result<Frame> {
  643|   113k|    let coded_w = sps.width_mbs * 16;
  644|   113k|    let coded_h = sps.height_mbs * 16;

  652|   113k|        .checked_sub((ct + cb_) * 2)
  653|   113k|        .ok_or(Error::BadValue("crop height"))?;
                                                            ^0

  655|   113k|    let (x0, y0) = (cl as usize * 2, ct as usize * 2);
  656|   113k|    if w == 0 || h == 0 || x0 + w > buf.w || y0 + h > buf.h {
  657|      0|        return Err(Error::BadValue("h264 crop window outside frame"));
  658|   113k|    }
  659|   113k|    let cw = w.div_ceil(2);
  660|   113k|    let ch = h.div_ceil(2);

  663|   113k|        height: h as u32,

  667|   113k|    };
  668|  3.22M|    for r in 0..h {
                           ^113k

  671|  3.22M|    }

  673|  1.61M|    for r in 0..ch {
                           ^113k

  753|       |/// P_Skip reconstruction (spec 8.4.1 / 7.3.5.2): one 16x16 partition,
  754|       |/// `ref_idx` 0, MV = the median predictor, no residual.
  755|  95.5k|fn p_skip(pic: &mut Pic, cx: &mut SliceCx<'_>, dpb: &Dpb, idx: usize, wm: usize) -> Result<()> {
  756|  95.5k|    let map = MbMap {
  757|  95.5k|        idx,
  758|  95.5k|        x: idx % wm,
  759|  95.5k|        y: idx / wm,
  760|  95.5k|        width: wm,
  761|  95.5k|        sid: cx.slice_id,
  762|  95.5k|    };
  763|  95.5k|    if cx.ref_order.is_empty() {
  764|      0|        return Err(Error::BadValue("P_Skip with empty ref list"));
  765|  95.5k|    }
  766|       |    // Spec 8.4.1.1: the skip motion vector is the median predictor,
  767|       |    // EXCEPT it is forced to (0,0) when the left or top neighbour is
  768|       |    // unavailable (frame edge or another slice) or when either one's
  769|       |    // reference-0 vector is already (0,0).
  770|  95.5k|    let a = mv_at(&pic.mbs, None, map, -1, 0);
  771|  95.5k|    let b = mv_at(&pic.mbs, None, map, 0, -1);
  772|   100k|    let force_zero = |n: Option<([i16; 2], i32)>| match n {
                      ^95.5k
  773|  69.3k|        None => true,

 1798|       |/// MVP for one partition (spec 8.4.1.3): directional special cases

 1801|       |#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
 1802|  2.65k|fn mvp_l0_parts(
 1803|  2.65k|    mbs: &[MbState],

    1|       |//! Decoded picture buffer: reference marking (sliding window + MMCO
    2|       |//! ops 1–4), PicNum derivation, and RefPicList0 initialisation plus
    3|       |//! slice reordering (spec 8.2.4).
    4|       |//!
    5|       |//! Short-term and long-term frames are tracked: MMCO op 3 moves a
    6|       |//! short-term picture to a long-term slot (spec 8.2.5.4.3, including
    7|       |//! the same-`LongTermFrameIdx` displacement and sliding-window
    8|       |//! interplay), and long-term pictures join the reference lists after
    9|       |//! every short-term picture. The remaining long-term assignments —
   10|       |//! MMCO op 6 (current picture to long-term), op 4 caps and long-term
   11|       |//! list reordering — surface as [`Error::Unsupported`] rather than
   12|       |//! silently marking wrong, as does `long_term_reference_flag` on IDR
   13|       |//! (refused at parse).

  443|       |    /// Minimal P-slice header with the given marking command list.
  444|     11|    fn marking_header(adaptive: bool, mmco: Vec<Mmco>) -> SliceHeader {

    1|       |//! MSB-first bit reader with Exp-Golomb (`ue`/`se`/`te`/`me`) decoding.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! The reader keeps a 64-bit lookahead buffer so VLC decoding can peek a
    4|       |//! fixed window before committing. Every read bounds-checks: truncation
    5|       |//! and oversized code words surface as [`Error`], never as a panic or a
    6|       |//! wrapped shift. H.264's Exp-Golomb `ue(v)` is unbounded in theory, but
    7|       |//! the syntax caps every use well below 32 bits, so [`Br::ue`] rejects
    8|       |//! prefixes longer than 32 zeros.
    9|       |
   10|       |use pith_digest::{Error, Result};
   11|       |
   12|       |/// Bit-level reader over an RBSP byte slice (emulation prevention already
   13|       |/// removed). Bit order is the H.264 convention: bit 0 is the MSB of
   14|       |/// byte 0.
   15|       |#[derive(Debug)]
   16|       |pub(crate) struct Br<'a> {
   17|       |    #[cfg(test)]
   18|       |    pub(crate) data: &'a [u8],
   19|       |    #[cfg(not(test))]
   20|       |    data: &'a [u8],
   21|       |    /// Index of the next bit to read, counting from the MSB of byte 0.
   22|       |    pos: usize,
   23|       |}
   24|       |
   25|       |impl<'a> Br<'a> {
   26|       |    /// Creates a reader over `data`, positioned at bit 0.

   28|   188k|        Br { data, pos: 0 }
   29|   188k|    }

   34|  56.9M|    }

   77|       |    /// Peeks the next `n` bits (`n <= 32`) without consuming them.
   78|       |    /// The first stream bit is the MSB of the returned value.

    1|       |//! Inter prediction for P slices: quarter-pel luma interpolation via
    2|       |//! the 6-tap filter (spec 8.4.2.2.1, Tables 8-11/8-12), eighth-pel
    3|       |//! chroma bilinear (8.4.2.2.2), and the weighted-prediction stage
    4|       |//! (8.4.3).
    5|       |//!
    6|       |//! All interpolation is pure over the reference plane plus the motion
    7|       |//! vector; out-of-picture fetches clamp to the reference border per
    8|       |//! spec 8.4.2.2.1's `Clip3` on the integer taps.
    9|       |
   10|       |/// Clamped integer sample fetch from a `width`×`height` plane.
   11|   771M|fn px(ref_: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize, x: i32, y: i32) -> i32 {
   12|   771M|    let xc = x.clamp(0, w as i32 - 1) as usize;
   13|   771M|    let yc = y.clamp(0, h as i32 - 1) as usize;
   14|   771M|    i32::from(ref_[yc * w + xc])
   15|   771M|}
   16|       |
   17|   184M|fn clip8(v: i32) -> u8 {
   18|   184M|    v.clamp(0, 255) as u8
   19|   184M|}
   20|       |
   21|       |/// Horizontal 6-tap intermediate (unclipped `b1`-style value) centered
   22|       |/// at half position (x + ½, y): taps integer columns x−2..x+3.
   23|  36.4M|fn h_tap(ref_: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize, x: i32, y: i32) -> i32 {
   24|  36.4M|    px(ref_, w, h, x - 2, y) - 5 * px(ref_, w, h, x - 1, y)

   92|       |/// One chroma prediction sample (spec 8.4.2.2.2): bilinear with
   93|       |/// eighth-pel weights, rounding constant 32 >> 6.
   94|  47.1M|fn chroma_sample(ref_: &[u8], w: usize, h: usize, x: i32, y: i32, fx: i32, fy: i32) -> u8 {
   95|  47.1M|    let a = px(ref_, w, h, x, y);
   96|  47.1M|    let b = px(ref_, w, h, x + 1, y);
   97|  47.1M|    let c = px(ref_, w, h, x, y + 1);
   98|  47.1M|    let d = px(ref_, w, h, x + 1, y + 1);
   99|  47.1M|    let v =
  100|  47.1M|        ((8 - fx) * (8 - fy) * a + fx * (8 - fy) * b + (8 - fx) * fy * c + fx * fy * d + 32) >> 6;
  101|  47.1M|    clip8(v)
  102|  47.1M|}

  104|       |/// Motion-compensates one luma partition of `pw`×`ph` samples whose
  105|       |/// top-left corner sits at `(px0, py0)` in picture coordinates and whose
  106|       |/// quarter-pel motion vector is `mv`. Writes into `dst` (row-major,
  107|       |/// `pw` stride = `pw`).

  109|   683k|pub(crate) fn mc_luma(

  114|   683k|    py0: i32,

  117|   683k|    ph: usize,

  119|   683k|) {

  121|   683k|    let mvy = mv.1;

                           ^683k
  123|  94.3M|        for x in 0..pw {
                               ^7.51M

  125|  94.3M|            // xIntL = xAL + (mvx >> 2), xFracL = mvx & 3.
  126|  94.3M|            let ax = px0 + x as i32;
  127|  94.3M|            let ay = py0 + y as i32;

  130|  94.3M|            let fx = mvx & 3;
  131|  94.3M|            let fy = mvy & 3;

  133|  94.3M|        }

  135|   683k|}
  136|       |
  137|       |/// Chroma counterpart of [`mc_luma`]: `mv` is the *luma* vector; the

    1|       |//! Intra prediction (spec 8.3): Intra-4x4's nine modes,
    2|       |//! Intra-16x16's four, and the 8x8 chroma modes. All functions are
    3|       |//! pure: the caller gathers the neighbouring samples (with their
    4|       |//! availability flags) and every function here is a closed-form
    5|       |//! expression over them.
    6|       |
    7|       |use pith_digest::{Error, Result};

    9|       |/// The neighbour samples one 4x4 (or 8x8/16x16) block's predictor is
   10|       |/// built from. `top_right` is only fetched for modes that need it.
   11|       |#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
   12|       |pub(crate) struct NbSamples {
   13|       |    /// `p[-1, 0..n)` left column (n = block size).
   14|       |    pub left: [u8; 16],
   15|       |    /// `p[0..n, -1]` top row.
   16|       |    pub top: [u8; 16],
   17|       |    /// `p[n..2n, -1]` top-right row continuation, when available.
   18|       |    pub top_right: Option<[u8; 16]>,
   19|       |    /// `p[-1,-1]` top-left corner, when available.
   20|       |    pub top_left: Option<u8>,
   21|       |    /// Left column available.
   22|       |    pub has_left: bool,
   23|       |    /// Top row available.
   24|       |    pub has_top: bool,

   33|  2.77M|pub(crate) fn pred4x4(mode: u8, nb: &NbSamples, out: &mut [u8; 16]) -> Result<()> {
   34|  2.77M|    let t = &nb.top[..4];

  282|       |/// DC mean over the available sides of `nb.top[..n]`/`nb.left[..n]`,
  283|       |/// used both for mode 0 and as the substitution when a required side
  284|       |/// is missing (non-conformant streams still decode).
  285|  10.2k|fn dc_mean(top: &[u8], left: &[u8], has_top: bool, has_left: bool) -> u8 {
  286|  10.2k|    let n = top.len().max(left.len()) as u32;
  287|  10.2k|    match (has_left, has_top) {
  288|       |        (true, true) => {
  289|      0|            ((left.iter().map(|&s| u32::from(s)).sum::<u32>()
  290|      0|                + top.iter().map(|&s| u32::from(s)).sum::<u32>()
  291|      0|                + n)
  292|      0|                >> (2 * n.trailing_zeros())) as u8
  293|       |        }
  294|       |        (true, false) => {
  295|  19.7k|            ((left.iter().map(|&s| u32::from(s)).sum::<u32>() + n / 2) >> n.trailing_zeros()) as u8
                            ^2.15k^2.15k ^2.15k                 ^2.15k         ^2.15k    ^2.15k^2.15k
  296|       |        }
  297|       |        (false, true) => {
  298|  64.8k|            ((top.iter().map(|&s| u32::from(s)).sum::<u32>() + n / 2) >> n.trailing_zeros()) as u8
                            ^7.81k^7.81k ^7.81k                 ^7.81k         ^7.81k    ^7.81k^7.81k
  299|       |        }
  300|    323|        (false, false) => 128,
  301|       |    }

  304|       |/// Intra-16x16 prediction (spec 8.3.3, Table 8-3): modes
  305|       |/// 0 = Vertical, 1 = Horizontal, 2 = DC, 3 = Plane — **not** the
  306|       |/// chroma order (which puts DC first). A mode whose required side is
  307|       |/// unavailable (stream edge or unconstrained-intra inter neighbour)
  308|       |/// falls back to the DC mean of whichever side exists — matching
  309|       |/// reference-decoder behaviour — rather than faulting the stream.
  310|  18.6k|pub(crate) fn pred16x16(mode: u8, nb: &NbSamples, out: &mut [u8; 256]) -> Result<()> {
  311|  18.6k|    match mode {

                                              ^2.72k        ^2.67k      ^2.67k
  348|    194|                out.fill(dc_mean(
  349|    194|                    &nb.top[..16],

  397|       |/// 8x8 chroma prediction (spec 8.3.4): mode 0 DC (per-quadrant means
  398|       |/// with per-side fallback), 1 horizontal, 2 vertical, 3 plane.
  399|   392k|pub(crate) fn pred_chroma(mode: u8, nb: &NbSamples, out: &mut [u8; 64]) -> Result<()> {
  400|   392k|    match mode {
  401|       |        0 => {
  402|       |            // Spec 8.3.4.2 — asymmetric quadrants:
  403|       |            //   Q0 (x<4, y<4): mean of top[0..4] + left[0..4]
  404|       |            //   Q1 (x>=4, y<4): top[4..8] alone (left side unused)
  405|       |            //   Q2 (x<4, y>=4): left[4..8] alone (top side unused)

  409|       |            // and a quadrant with neither side uses 128.

  513|       |                } else {

  515|       |                };
  516|   117k|                let lv = if x == 4 {
  517|  29.2k|                    i32::from(nb.top_left.unwrap())
  518|       |                } else {
  519|  87.7k|                    i32::from(nb.left[3 - x])
  520|       |                };
  521|   117k|                v += x as i32 * (hv - lv);
  522|       |            }
  523|  29.2k|            let a = 16 * (i32::from(nb.left[7]) + i32::from(nb.top[7]));
  524|  29.2k|            let b = (17 * h + 16) >> 5;
  525|  29.2k|            let c = (17 * v + 16) >> 5;
  526|   234k|            for y in 0..8 {
                                   ^29.2k
  527|  1.87M|                for x in 0..8 {
                                       ^234k
  528|  1.87M|                    out[y * 8 + x] = clip((a + b * (x as i32 - 3) + c * (y as i32 - 3) + 16) >> 5);
  529|  1.87M|                }
  530|       |            }
  531|       |        }
  532|      0|        _ => return Err(Error::BadValue("chroma mode over 3")),
  533|       |    }
  534|   383k|    Ok(())
  535|   392k|}
  536|       |
  537|       |/// The nine Intra-8x8 modes (spec 8.3.5.2) over 64 output samples in
  538|       |/// raster order. The reference decoder's `pred8x8l` family: neighbour
  539|       |/// samples are *low-pass filtered* before use (`(a + 2b + c + 2) >> 2`
  540|       |/// with edge replication at the ends and `has_topleft`/`has_topright`
  541|       |/// substituting the nearest real sample). Unavailable inputs make the
  542|       |/// mode fall back to DC or 128, matching the spec's
  543|       |/// `check_intra4x4_pred_mode` legalisation.
  544|       |#[allow(clippy::too_many_lines)]
  545|  18.1k|pub(crate) fn pred8x8(mode: u8, nb: &NbSamples, out: &mut [u8; 64]) {
  546|       |    // Raw neighbours.
  547|   145k|    let l_raw: [i32; 8] = core::array::from_fn(|i| i32::from(nb.left[i]));
                      ^18.1k ^18.1k     ^18.1k
  548|   145k|    let t_raw: [i32; 8] = core::array::from_fn(|i| i32::from(nb.top[i]));
                      ^18.1k ^18.1k     ^18.1k
  549|  18.1k|    let tr_raw: [i32; 8] = nb
  550|  18.1k|        .top_right
  551|  80.1k|        .map(|a| core::array::from_fn(|i| i32::from(a[i])))
                       ^18.1k  ^10.0k
  552|  18.1k|        .unwrap_or([i32::from(nb.top[7]); 8]);
  553|  18.1k|    let tl = nb.top_left.map(i32::from);
  554|  18.1k|    let has_tl = tl.is_some();
  555|  18.1k|    let has_l = nb.has_left;
  556|  18.1k|    let has_t = nb.has_top;
  557|  18.1k|    let has_tr = nb.top_right.is_some();
  558|       |
  559|       |    // Filtered left samples l0..l7 (spec 8.3.5.2.1 `p'[-1, y]`).
  560|  18.1k|    let mut l = [0i32; 8];
  561|  18.1k|    l[0] = (tl.unwrap_or(l_raw[0]) + 2 * l_raw[0] + l_raw[1] + 2) >> 2;
  562|   108k|    for i in 1..7 {
                           ^18.1k
  563|   108k|        l[i] = (l_raw[i - 1] + 2 * l_raw[i] + l_raw[i + 1] + 2) >> 2;
  564|   108k|    }
  565|  18.1k|    l[7] = (l_raw[6] + 3 * l_raw[7] + 2) >> 2;
  566|       |
  567|       |    // Filtered top samples t0..t15 (`p'[x, -1]`, x = 0..15).
  568|  18.1k|    let mut t = [0i32; 16];
  569|  18.1k|    t[0] = (tl.unwrap_or(t_raw[0]) + 2 * t_raw[0] + t_raw[1] + 2) >> 2;
  570|   108k|    for i in 1..7 {
                           ^18.1k
  571|   108k|        t[i] = (t_raw[i - 1] + 2 * t_raw[i] + t_raw[i + 1] + 2) >> 2;
  572|   108k|    }
  573|  18.1k|    t[7] = (tr_raw[0] + 2 * t_raw[7] + t_raw[6] + 2) >> 2;
  574|  18.1k|    if has_tr {
  575|  70.1k|        for i in 8..15 {
                               ^10.0k
  576|       |            // PTR(8) reads SRC(7,-1) — the *unfiltered* last top
  577|       |            // sample — not filtered t[7] (ffmpeg pred8x8l).
  578|  70.1k|            let a = if i == 8 { t_raw[7] } else { tr_raw[i - 9] };
                                              ^10.0k            ^60.0k
  579|  70.1k|            t[i] = (a + 2 * tr_raw[i - 8] + tr_raw[i - 7] + 2) >> 2;
  580|       |        }
  581|  10.0k|        t[15] = (tr_raw[6] + 3 * tr_raw[7] + 2) >> 2;
  582|       |    } else {
  583|  65.1k|        for tv in t.iter_mut().skip(8) {
                                ^8.14k^8.14k     ^8.14k
  584|  65.1k|            *tv = t_raw[7];
  585|  65.1k|        }
  586|       |    }
  587|       |    // Filtered top-left corner `p'[-1, -1]`.
  588|  18.1k|    let lt = (l_raw[0] + 2 * tl.unwrap_or_else(|| t_raw[0].max(l_raw[0])) + t_raw[0] + 2) >> 2;
                                                                ^4.03k   ^4.03k^4.03k
  589|       |
  590|   854k|    let set = |out: &mut [u8; 64], x: usize, y: usize, v: i32| {
                      ^18.1k
  591|   854k|        out[y * 8 + x] = clip(v);
  592|   854k|    };
  593|       |
  594|  18.1k|    if !has_l && !has_t {
                               ^2.02k
  595|    260|        out.fill(128);
  596|    260|        return;
  597|  17.9k|    }
  598|  17.9k|    match mode {
  599|       |        // Intra_8x8_Vertical.
  600|       |        0 => {
  601|  4.23k|            if !has_t {
  602|     26|                let dc = ((l.iter().sum::<i32>() + 4) >> 3) as u8;
  603|     26|                out.fill(dc);
  604|     26|                return;
  605|  4.21k|            }
  606|  33.6k|            for y in 0..8 {
                                   ^4.21k
  607|   269k|                for (x, &tv) in t.iter().enumerate().take(8) {
                                              ^33.6k^33.6k ^33.6k      ^33.6k
  608|   269k|                    set(out, x, y, tv);
  609|   269k|                }
  610|       |            }
  611|       |        }
  612|       |        // Intra_8x8_Horizontal.
  613|       |        1 => {
  614|  3.03k|            if !has_l {

  616|     40|                out.fill(dc);
  617|     40|                return;
  618|  2.99k|            }

                                          ^2.99k^2.99k ^2.99k      ^2.99k
  620|   191k|                for x in 0..8 {
                                       ^23.9k
  621|   191k|                    set(out, x, y, lv);
  622|   191k|                }
  623|       |            }
  624|       |        }
  625|       |        // Intra_8x8_DC (spec 8-64: (sum t' + sum l' + 8) >> 4).
  626|       |        2 => {

                                               ^3.12k

  631|       |            } else {
  632|  1.07k|                ((t[..8].iter().sum::<i32>() + 4) >> 3) as u8
  633|       |            };
  634|  4.20k|            out.fill(dc);
  635|       |        }
  636|       |        // Intra_8x8_Diagonal_Down_Left (spec 8-65): top + top-right,
  637|       |        // requires `has_t`. The t7..t15 chain extends when top-right
  638|       |        // is real; with it replicated the formula degenerates to
  639|       |        // ffmpeg's `SRC(7,-1)` replication.
  640|       |        3 => {

  643|     24|                out.fill(dc);
  644|     24|                return;
  645|    784|            }
  646|  6.27k|            for y in 0..8 {
                                   ^784

  649|    784|                        (t[14] + 3 * t[15] + 2) >> 2
  650|       |                    } else {
  651|  49.3k|                        (t[x + y] + 2 * t[x + y + 1] + t[x + y + 2] + 2) >> 2
  652|       |                    };
  653|  50.1k|                    set(out, x, y, v);
  654|       |                }
  655|       |            }
  656|       |        }
  657|       |        // Intra_8x8_Diagonal_Down_Right (spec 8-66): left + top +

 1020|       |/// Shared DC fallback for the tl-dependent modes.
 1021|    171|fn dc_fallback(out: &mut [u8; 64], l: &[i32; 8], t: &[i32; 16], has_l: bool, has_t: bool) {

C:\Users\n24q02m-wpc\projects\pith-h264\src\lib.rs:
    1|       |//! H.264/AVC **baseline-profile** decoding: Annex-B NAL splitting,
    2|       |//! SPS/PPS, I- and P-slice macroblock layers, CAVLC entropy decoding,
    3|       |//! the 4x4 integer transform (plus the Intra-16x16 luma-DC and chroma-DC
    4|       |//! Hadamard transforms), intra prediction (4x4 and 16x16 luma, 8x8
    5|       |//! chroma), P-slice motion compensation with quarter-sample luma
    6|       |//! (6-tap) and eighth-sample chroma interpolation, and the in-loop
    7|       |//! deblocking filter.
    8|       |//!
    9|       |//! Part of the `pith` zero-dependency hashing suite: every crate in
   10|       |//! this suite builds without a single registry package, so the
   11|       |//! whole suite resolves offline.
   12|       |//!
   13|       |//! # Scope
   14|       |//!
   15|       |//! The decoder accepts Baseline-profile streams (`profile_idc` 66) and
   16|       |//! the Baseline feature subset of Constrained Baseline (66 with the
   17|       |//! constraint flags), Main (77) and Extended (88) — Main/Extended are
   18|       |//! parsed because their streams frequently exercise no feature beyond
   19|       |//! CAVLC + I/P slices. Syntax elements outside that subset surface as
   20|       |//! [`Error::Unsupported`] with the offending feature named:
   21|       |//!
   22|       |//! * `entropy_coding_mode_flag` (CABAC), B/SP/SI slices, `transform_8x8`
   23|       |//!   (High-profile 8x8 transform), `chroma_format_idc != 1` and every
   24|       |//!   High+ `profile_idc` (100, 110, 122, 244, 44, 83, 86, 118, 128)
   25|       |//!   plus unknown values, named by profile name;
   26|       |//! * field pictures, MBAFF and `frame_mbs_only_flag == 0` altogether;
   27|       |//! * FMO (`num_slice_groups_minus1 > 0`), arbitrary slice order and
   28|       |//!   slice groups maping is rejected;
   29|       |//! * `redundant_pic_cnt_present_flag`, data partitioning (DPA/B/C NALs);
   30|       |//! * long-term reference *assignment* beyond MMCO op 3: MMCO 4/6 and
   31|       |//!   `long_term_reference_flag` on IDR error out rather than guess
   32|       |//!   (op 3's short-term→long-term move is supported, spec
   33|       |//!   8.2.5.4.3).
   34|       |//!
   35|       |//! The stream must be Annex-B framed; `avcC` length-prefixed NALs are
   36|       |//! `pith_mp4`'s demux concern, not this crate's.
   37|       |//!
   38|       |//! Decoding is deterministic and single-threaded by construction; the
   39|       |//! output is the reconstructed picture as decoded, with frame cropping
   40|       |//! applied per SPS.
   41|       |
   42|       |#![no_std]
   43|       |#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
   44|       |#![deny(missing_docs)]
   45|       |

   91|       |pub use sps::{Profile, Sps};
   92|       |
   93|       |/// One decoded output picture, 8-bit 4:2:0, after SPS cropping.
   94|       |///
   95|       |/// `stride`-free layout: each plane is `width*height` samples for `y`
   96|       |/// and `(width/2)*(height/2)` for `cb`/`cr`, row-major, no padding.
   97|       |/// Cropping (SPS `frame_cropping_*`) is applied so the dimensions are
   98|       |/// the *displayed* size, which for the odd-width cases the conformance
   99|       |/// suite probes can differ from the coded size.
  100|       |#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
  101|       |pub struct Frame {
  102|       |    /// Displayed width in luma samples.
  103|       |    pub width: u32,
  104|       |    /// Displayed height in luma samples.
  105|       |    pub height: u32,
  106|       |    /// Luma plane, `width * height` bytes.
  107|       |    pub y: Vec<u8>,
  108|       |    /// Cb plane, `(width/2) * (height/2)` bytes.
  109|       |    pub cb: Vec<u8>,
  110|       |    /// Cr plane, `(width/2) * (height/2)` bytes.
  111|       |    pub cr: Vec<u8>,
  112|       |}
  113|       |
  114|       |/// Ceilings a caller imposes on one decode call.
  115|       |///
  116|       |/// [`Limits`] is not optional: H.264's parse tables let a two-line
  117|       |/// bitstream demand a gigapixel frame buffer, so decoding without a
  118|       |/// ceiling is a denial-of-service primitive. [`Default`] covers the
  119|       |/// suite's hashing use — streams up to 64 MiB producing pictures up to
  120|       |/// 8192x8192 and at most 1024 pictures — and is *not* a permissive
  121|       |/// mode: exceeding a bound returns [`Error::TooLarge`].
  122|       |#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
  123|       |pub struct Limits {
  124|       |    /// Hard ceiling on input consumed, in bytes. Default 64 MiB.
  125|       |    pub max_input: usize,
  126|       |    /// Hard ceiling on `width * height` of any one picture in luma
  127|       |    /// samples (the coded size, before cropping). Default 33,554,432
  128|       |    /// (8192x4096, level 5.2 territory).
  129|       |    pub max_luma_samples: usize,
  130|       |    /// Hard ceiling on the number of frames one [`decode`] call
  131|       |    /// produces. Default 1024.
  132|       |    pub max_frames: usize,
  133|       |    /// Hard ceiling on `max_num_ref_frames` honoured in the DPB; the
  134|       |    /// stream's own cap still applies (so a level-4 stream is limited
  135|       |    /// by the smaller of the two). Default 16.
  136|       |    pub max_refs: u32,
  137|       |}
  138|       |
  139|       |impl Default for Limits {
  140|  21.6k|    fn default() -> Self {
  141|  21.6k|        Limits {
  142|  21.6k|            max_input: 64 * 1024 * 1024,
  143|  21.6k|            max_luma_samples: 8192 * 4096,
  144|  21.6k|            max_frames: 1024,
  145|  21.6k|            max_refs: 16,
  146|  21.6k|        }

    1|       |//! Macroblock model: types, per-MB decode state, and the neighbour
    2|       |//! geometry every context-derivation step shares.
    3|       |//!
    4|       |//! Two coordinate conventions coexist in the spec and both appear here:
    5|       |//!
    6|       |//! * **4x4 raster index** `r = x4 + 4*y4` — the order intra-4x4 mode
    7|       |//!   parsing and reconstruction use;
    8|       |//! * **8x8-group-major index** `b` (see [`crate::tables::block_index`])
    9|       |//!   — the order `coded_block_pattern`, CAVLC `nC` derivation, MV
   10|       |//!   storage and deblocking boundary strengths use. All per-4x4 arrays
   11|       |//!   in [`MbState`] are group-major.
   12|       |

  319|       |    /// `(w4, h4)` of each sub-partition in 4x4 units, and the count.
  320|       |    /// For `BDirect` this returns the single whole-8x8 partition; the
  321|       |    /// decoder may split it into 4x4 under `direct_8x8_inference`.
  322|   104k|    pub(crate) fn parts(self) -> ([(u8, u8); 4], usize) {
  323|   104k|        match self {
  324|  77.6k|            SubMbType::S8x8 => ([(2, 2), (0, 0), (0, 0), (0, 0)], 1),
  325|  9.88k|            SubMbType::S8x4 => ([(2, 1), (2, 1), (0, 0), (0, 0)], 2),
  326|  8.59k|            SubMbType::S4x8 => ([(1, 2), (1, 2), (0, 0), (0, 0)], 2),
  327|  7.96k|            SubMbType::S4x4 => ([(1, 1); 4], 4),
  328|      0|            SubMbType::BDirect => ([(2, 2); 4], 1),
  329|      0|            SubMbType::BInter { w4, h4, nparts, .. } => {
  330|      0|                let mut p = [(0u8, 0u8); 4];
  331|      0|                for e in p.iter_mut().take(nparts as usize) {
  332|      0|                    *e = (w4, h4);
  333|      0|                }
  334|      0|                (p, nparts as usize)
  335|       |            }
  336|       |        }
  337|   104k|    }

  339|       |
  340|       |/// Per-macroblock decoded state, shared by prediction, CAVLC `nC` and
  341|       |/// deblocking. All per-4x4 arrays are 8x8-group-major
  342|       |/// ([`crate::tables::block_index`]).
  343|       |#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
  344|       |pub(crate) struct MbState {
  345|       |    /// Macroblock type.
  346|       |    pub mb_type: MbType,
  347|       |    /// Slice this macroblock belongs to (for cross-slice checks).
  348|       |    pub slice_id: u32,
  349|       |    /// Debug: raster index of this MB (set by the decoder loop).
  350|       |    pub dbg_idx: u32,
  351|       |    /// `QPy` for this macroblock.
  352|       |    pub qp_y: u8,
  353|       |    /// Deblocking idc of the slice that coded this MB (needed when
  354|       |    /// neighbouring MBs live in different slices).
  355|       |    pub disable_deblock_idc: u8,
  356|       |    /// `alpha`/`beta` deblock offsets of the coding slice.
  357|       |    pub filter_offset_a: i8,
  358|       |    /// `beta` offset.
  359|       |    pub filter_offset_b: i8,
  360|       |    /// `total_coeff` per 4x4 luma block (group-major, 24 entries cover
  361|       |    /// 16 luma + 8 chroma AC, in the same group-major order for the
  362|       |    /// chroma 8x8 pair) — used by `nC` prediction and deblocking.
  363|       |    /// Under `transform_size_8x8` each 8x8 group's four entries all
  364|       |    /// carry the 8x8 `TotalCoeff` (spec 8.7.2.1's per-8x8 `nz` view).
  365|       |    pub nz: [u8; 24],
  366|       |    /// Luma motion vectors per 4x4 block for list L0 (quarter-pel).
  367|       |    pub mv: [[i16; 2]; 16],
  368|       |    /// `ref_idx_l0` per 4x4 block (0xff = not used on this list).
  369|       |    pub ref_idx: [u8; 16],
  370|       |    /// True once this block's L0 MV has been decoded/committed. `ref_idx`
  371|       |    /// may be committed early (the whole sub-mb's refs decode before any
  372|       |    /// mvd), so availability for MV prediction needs a separate gate —
  373|       |    /// an early ref with a stale zero MV must read as *unavailable*.
  374|       |    pub mv_valid: [bool; 16],
  375|       |    /// Luma motion vectors per 4x4 block for list L1 (B slices).
  376|       |    pub mv_l1: [[i16; 2]; 16],
  377|       |    /// `ref_idx_l1` per 4x4 block (0xff = unused).
  378|       |    pub ref_idx_l1: [u8; 16],
  379|       |    /// Parsed intra-4x4 modes (raster order), 0..8 or 0xff unset.
  380|       |    pub i4x4_modes: [u8; 16],
  381|       |    /// `intra_chroma_pred_mode` 0..=3.
  382|       |    pub chroma_pred: u8,
  383|       |    /// `mb_skip_flag` (P_Skip or B_Skip; `BDirect` does NOT count as
  384|       |    /// skipped for the CABAC `mb_skip_flag` context derivation).
  385|       |    pub skip: bool,
  386|       |    /// `transform_size_8x8_flag` of this macroblock.
  387|       |    pub transform8x8: bool,
  388|       |    /// `CodedBlockPatternLuma` (4 bits) + `CodedBlockPatternChroma`
  389|       |    /// (2 bits) as parsed: `cbp = chroma<<4 | luma` — the CABAC
  390|       |    /// `coded_block_pattern` context and ffmpeg's `cbp_table` layout.
  391|       |    pub cbp: u8,
  392|       |    /// Coded-DC flags: bit0 = luma DC coded (i16x16 MB), bit1 = Cb DC,
  393|       |    /// bit2 = Cr DC — feeds the cat-0/3 `coded_block_flag` contexts.
  394|       |    pub dc_coded: u8,
  395|       |    /// `direct_spatial_mv_pred` in effect for this MB's direct blocks
  396|       |    /// (0 = temporal).
  397|       |    pub direct_spatial: bool,
  398|       |    /// Per-8x8-group direct-prediction flags (bit g = group g is
  399|       |    /// `B_Direct_8x8` or a whole-MB direct type) — the CABAC `ref_idx`
  400|       |    /// context checks them.
  401|       |    pub direct_mask: u8,
  402|       |    /// Absolute `mvd_l0` per 4x4 block clamped to <=70 (u8): the CABAC
  403|       |    /// `mvd` context sums these for the left/top neighbours.
  404|       |    pub mvd_l0: [[u8; 2]; 16],
  405|       |    /// Absolute `mvd_l1` per 4x4 block (B slices).
  406|       |    pub mvd_l1: [[u8; 2]; 16],
  407|       |}
  408|       |
  409|       |impl MbState {
  410|       |    /// Fresh state for an undecoded macroblock.
  411|   558k|    pub(crate) fn new() -> MbState {
  412|   558k|        MbState {
  413|   558k|            mb_type: MbType::PSkip,
  414|   558k|            slice_id: u32::MAX,
  415|   558k|            dbg_idx: u32::MAX,
  416|   558k|            qp_y: 26,
  417|   558k|            disable_deblock_idc: 0,
  418|   558k|            filter_offset_a: 0,
  419|   558k|            filter_offset_b: 0,
  420|   558k|            nz: [0; 24],
  421|   558k|            mv: [[0; 2]; 16],
  422|   558k|            ref_idx: [0xff; 16],
  423|   558k|            mv_valid: [false; 16],
  424|   558k|            mv_l1: [[0; 2]; 16],
  425|   558k|            ref_idx_l1: [0xff; 16],
  426|   558k|            i4x4_modes: [0xff; 16],
  427|   558k|            chroma_pred: 0,
  428|   558k|            skip: false,
  429|   558k|            transform8x8: false,
  430|   558k|            cbp: 0,
  431|   558k|            dc_coded: 0,
  432|   558k|            direct_spatial: false,
  433|   558k|            direct_mask: 0,

  435|   558k|            mvd_l1: [[0; 2]; 16],
  436|   558k|        }
  437|   558k|    }

  439|       |
  440|       |/// Position of the MB, 4x4-grid coordinates of its top-left sample and
  441|       |/// neighbour MB indices within a `mb_width` × `mb_height` map.
  442|       |#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug)]
  443|       |pub(crate) struct MbMap {
  444|       |    /// MB raster index.
  445|       |    pub idx: usize,
  446|       |    /// `mb_x`, `mb_y` in macroblocks.
  447|       |    pub x: usize,
  448|       |    /// Row.
  449|       |    pub y: usize,
  450|       |    /// Picture width in MBs.
  451|       |    pub width: usize,
  452|       |    /// Slice sequence number of the slice currently being decoded.
  453|       |    /// Foreign MBs count as neighbours only when their `slice_id`
  454|       |    /// matches — spec 7.4.3 / 9.2.1: blocks in a different slice are
  455|       |    /// not available as prediction context.
  456|       |    pub sid: u32,
  457|       |}
  458|       |
  459|       |impl MbMap {
  460|       |    /// Index of the left neighbour MB (mbAddrA), if any.
  461|  7.69M|    pub(crate) fn mb_a(self) -> Option<usize> {
  462|  7.69M|        if self.x > 0 { Some(self.idx - 1) } else { None }
                                      ^5.26M                      ^2.43M
  463|  7.69M|    }
  464|       |    /// Index of the top neighbour MB (mbAddrB), if any.
  465|  7.66M|    pub(crate) fn mb_b(self) -> Option<usize> {
  466|  7.66M|        if self.y > 0 {
  467|  5.16M|            Some(self.idx - self.width)
  468|       |        } else {
  469|  2.50M|            None
  470|       |        }
  471|  7.66M|    }
  472|       |    /// Index of the top-right neighbour MB (mbAddrC), if any.
  473|   410k|    pub(crate) fn mb_c(self) -> Option<usize> {
  474|   410k|        if self.y > 0 && self.x + 1 < self.width {
                                       ^331k

  476|       |        } else {
  477|   163k|            None
  478|       |        }
  479|   410k|    }
  480|       |    /// Index of the top-left neighbour MB (mbAddrD), if any.
  481|   712k|    pub(crate) fn mb_d(self) -> Option<usize> {

                                       ^416k

  489|       |
  490|       |/// Neighbours of the *raster*-index 4x4 block `r` used by intra-4x4
  491|       |/// prediction (spec 6.4.11.4). Returns
  492|       |/// `(a, b, c, d)` where each is `(mb, x4, y4)` — `mb` is one of
  493|       |/// `NeighbourMb`. `None` means unavailable by construction.
  494|       |#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
  495|       |pub(crate) enum Nb {
  496|       |    /// Same macroblock.
  497|       |    Curr,
  498|       |    /// mbAddrA (left).
  499|       |    A,
  500|       |    /// mbAddrB (above).
  501|       |    B,
  502|       |    /// mbAddrC (above-right).
  503|       |    C,
  504|       |    /// mbAddrD (above-left).
  505|       |    D,
  506|       |    /// Unavailable (out of picture or not yet coded).
  507|       |    None,
  508|       |}
  509|       |
  510|       |/// `(mb, x4, y4)` triple naming a 4x4 block for prediction contexts.
  511|       |pub(crate) type BlockRef = (Nb, usize, usize);
  512|       |
  513|       |/// Neighbour 4x4 blocks of raster block `r` inside the MB
  514|       |/// (spec 6.4.11.4 + 8.3.1.1 availability by construction):
  515|       |/// `(A, B, C, D)` as luma-block coordinates.
  516|  5.59M|pub(crate) fn intra4x4_neighbours(blk: usize) -> [BlockRef; 4] {
  517|       |    // `blk` is the group-major (luma4x4BlkIdx) index. Neighbourhood is
  518|       |    // derived on the 4x4 raster grid, then mapped back to group-major
  519|       |    // indices so same-MB members carry `Curr` + group-major id.
  520|  5.59M|    let (x, y) = crate::tables::block_xy(blk);
  521|  5.59M|    let (xi, yi) = (x as i32, y as i32);
  522|  22.3M|    let map = |nx: i32, ny: i32| -> BlockRef {
                      ^5.59M
  523|  22.3M|        if (0..4).contains(&nx) && (0..4).contains(&ny) {
                                                  ^18.1M^18.1M   ^18.1M
  524|  14.6M|            (Nb::Curr, nx as usize, ny as usize)
  525|       |        } else {
  526|       |            // Foreign block: which neighbour MB by the crossed edges.
  527|  7.70M|            let who = match (nx < 0, ny < 0, nx >= 4) {
  528|  2.45M|                (true, false, false) => Nb::A,
  529|  3.50M|                (false, true, false) => Nb::B,
  530|   348k|                (false, true, true) => Nb::C,
  531|   353k|                (true, true, false) => Nb::D,
  532|      0|                (true, false, true) => Nb::A,
  533|  1.04M|                _ => Nb::None,
  534|       |            };
  535|  7.70M|            if who == Nb::A && nx < 0 && ny < 0 {
                                             ^2.45M    ^2.45M
  536|      0|                (Nb::D, 3, 3)
  537|       |            } else {
  538|  7.70M|                let lx = nx.rem_euclid(4);
  539|  7.70M|                let ly = ny.rem_euclid(4);
  540|  7.70M|                (who, lx as usize, ly as usize)
  541|       |            }
  542|       |        }
  543|  22.3M|    };
  544|  5.59M|    let a = map(xi - 1, yi);
  545|  5.59M|    let b = map(xi, yi - 1);
  546|  5.59M|    let c = map(xi + 1, yi - 1);
  547|  5.59M|    let d = map(xi - 1, yi - 1);
  548|  5.59M|    [a, b, c, d]
  549|  5.59M|}

  556|       |    /// the P/B tail ranges and its upper-bound rejection.

                              ^0

  561|       |        // pred 3, cbp_luma 15, cbp_chroma 2 (v%12/4).

                              ^0

  568|       |            })
  569|       |        ));
  570|      1|        assert!(matches!(
                              ^0

  681|       |    /// Sub-macroblock tables: P (4 codes) and B (13 codes), geometry
  682|       |    /// decomposition, and the upper-bound rejections.
  683|       |    #[test]
  684|      1|    fn sub_mb_tables_complete() {
  685|      1|        assert!(matches!(SubMbType::from_code(0), Ok(SubMbType::S8x8)));
                              ^0

                              ^0

                              ^0
  689|      1|        assert!(matches!(
                              ^0

  691|       |            Ok(SubMbType::BInter { .. })

    1|       |//! Annex-B NAL unit splitting and emulation-prevention removal.
    2|       |//!
    3|       |//! A H.264 byte stream is a run of start-code-prefixed NAL units
    4|       |//! (`00 00 01` or `00 00 00 01`). Inside each NAL's payload the encoder
    5|       |//! inserts `03` after `00 00` whenever the next byte would be `00`,
    6|       |//! `01`, `02` or `03`, so the RBSP is recovered by stripping every
    7|       |//! `00 00 03` triple (spec 7.4.1.1).
    8|       |
    9|       |use alloc::vec::Vec;
   10|       |
   11|       |use pith_digest::{Error, Result};
   12|       |
   13|       |/// NAL unit type codes (spec Table 7-1). Only the codes the decoder
   14|       |/// acts on get named constants.
   15|       |pub(crate) const NAL_SLICE: u8 = 1;
   16|       |/// DPA — partitioning is a baseline-legal feature this crate declines.
   17|       |pub(crate) const NAL_DPA: u8 = 2;
   18|       |/// DPB.
   19|       |pub(crate) const NAL_DPB: u8 = 3;
   20|       |/// DPC.
   21|       |pub(crate) const NAL_DPC: u8 = 4;
   22|       |/// IDR slice.
   23|       |pub(crate) const NAL_IDR: u8 = 5;
   24|       |/// SEI — skipped.
   25|       |pub(crate) const NAL_SEI: u8 = 6;
   26|       |/// Sequence parameter set.
   27|       |pub(crate) const NAL_SPS: u8 = 7;
   28|       |/// Picture parameter set.
   29|       |pub(crate) const NAL_PPS: u8 = 8;
   30|       |/// Access unit delimiter — skipped.
   31|       |pub(crate) const NAL_AUD: u8 = 9;
   32|       |/// End of sequence — flushes the last picture.
   33|       |pub(crate) const NAL_EOSEQ: u8 = 10;
   34|       |/// End of stream — flushes the last picture.
   35|       |pub(crate) const NAL_EOSTREAM: u8 = 11;
   36|       |/// Filler data — skipped.
   37|       |pub(crate) const NAL_FILLER: u8 = 12;
   38|       |/// SPS extension.
   39|       |pub(crate) const NAL_SPS_EXT: u8 = 13;
   40|       |/// Auxiliary / extension slices and reserved codes this crate declines.
   41|       |pub(crate) const NAL_CODED_SLICE_AUX: u8 = 19;
   42|       |/// Slice without partitioning is legal but beyond baseline scope here
   43|       |/// (extension/slice-aux and scalable types 14/15/20/21 are all routed
   44|       |/// to [`Error::Unsupported`]).
   45|       |///
   46|       |/// One Annex-B NAL unit: header fields plus the payload span in the
   47|       |/// source buffer (emulation prevention NOT yet removed).
   48|       |#[derive(Copy, Clone, Debug)]
   49|       |pub(crate) struct Nal<'a> {
   50|       |    /// `nal_ref_idc` (0..=3).
   51|       |    pub ref_idc: u8,
   52|       |    /// `nal_unit_type` (0..=31).
   53|       |    pub unit_type: u8,
   54|       |    /// Raw NAL payload including emulation-prevention bytes.
   55|       |    pub payload: &'a [u8],
   56|       |}
   57|       |
   58|       |/// Splits an Annex-B stream into NAL units.
   59|       |///
   60|       |/// `Err` when the stream contains no start code at all — a raw RBSP
   61|       |/// without Annex-B framing is not a byte stream. Trailing zero bytes
   62|       |/// after the last start code (or before the next one) are dropped from
   63|       |/// each payload, matching how decoders delimit the RBSP.

   75|       |    }

   78|    226|            what: "h264 Annex-B start code",
   79|    226|        });
   80|  33.5k|    }
   81|  33.5k|    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(starts.len());
   82|   246k|    for (s, &sc) in starts.iter().enumerate() {
                                  ^33.5k        ^33.5k
   83|   246k|        let mut begin = sc + 3;

   85|       |        // and the NAL header (a NAL header is never 0x00 itself).
   86|   246k|        if begin < data.len() && data[begin] == 0 {
                                               ^245k

   88|   245k|        }

   90|   212k|            Some(&n) => n,

   14|       |    /// constraint flags are checked separately).

   16|       |    /// `profile_idc` 77 (Main): CAVLC + CABAC, I/P/B slices.

   21|       |    /// transform. Scaling matrices, non-4:2:0 chroma and other bit
